- **Stacking:** a crit multiplies on top of the game's sneak attack bonus (bows and spears ×3, knives ×6, once per creature every 5 minutes on an unaware creature) and the stagger bonus (×2): a crit sneak shot with a bow ×6, a crit on a staggered creature ×4. Corruption's Backstab affix adds to the sneak bonus as before.
- **Unavoidable:** anything that isn't a creature's hit (falls, traps, burning and poison ticks, lava, players), hits the game's own dodge roll can't escape (`m_dodgeable` off: Bonemass's and the Gjall's and the hive's attacks, game dump), area blasts (the game's Aoe hits, and creatures' area attacks such as stomps and ground slams) and breath (creature attack items with "breath" in the name: Moder's and Fader's breath, the Bonemaw serpent, the fallen Valkyrie).
- **While you hold block,** a hit the block takes (blockable, from the front, a shield or weapon in hand) isn't rolled: the block or parry handles it, so the parry affixes keep working. A hit from behind or an unblockable one still rolls.
- **An avoided hit** does nothing at all (no damage, stagger, push or status effect): a floating "Dodge", "Avoid" or "Evade" (white) where it would have landed, and the game's dodge-roll whoosh (`Player.m_dodgeEffects`). No animation.
- **Testing:** `orlog_passive crit 100` (or `penetrate`, `dodge`, `avoid`, `evade`; any chance 0–100) sets a chance until you quit or type `orlog_passive off`; without arguments it lists your chances. With `devcommands` on, each passive that triggers also says so at the top left.

**How it works in multiplayer:**
- Critical Strike and Penetrate roll on the **attacker's game**, where the hit is made (`Character.Damage`), before Corruption's prefixes (Rallying and Skadi at High, life steal at Normal), so they count a crit's damage. The crit effect plays there.
- Resistances are applied on the **creature owner's game**. A Penetrate hit carries a marker in the hit's eitr field (`HitData.m_eitrAdd`, sent with every hit; vanilla only reads it above 0): `-1000 - original`. The owner's game takes it off before anything reads the hit and lifts the resistances in `HitData.ApplyResistance`.
- Dodge, Avoid and Evade roll on the **player's own game**, which owns the player and receives every hit on them (`Character.RPC_Damage`, first priority, so an avoided hit never reaches vanilla or Corruption's damage changes).
- Area hits are recognised on the player's game by their radius (`HitData.m_radius`, sent with every hit; vanilla only reads it on rocks). Aoe hits have one; the **creature's game** gives one to hits from its area attacks (`Attack.DoAreaAttack`) and breath projectiles.

Unit-tested (`OrlogPassiveTests`: the real table against D2's numbers, Penetrate's curve, the table matches the tree, chances by level, tooltip text, rolls, the avoid split, breath names, which resistances Penetrate lifts, the marker, bad tables).

**Known issues / not yet:**
- **Untested in-game.** Read in the cloud's copy of the game code (1.0.14); not checked against 1.0.21.
- **Breath by name:** found from the attack item's prefab name (`ItemData.m_dropPrefab`). If the game leaves that empty for creature attacks, breath can be avoided; the play-test checks Moder's breath.
- **PvP:** players' hits on each other are neither crit, penetrated nor avoided (creatures only).
- **No +skills yet:** the skill level is the points spent; the table stops at level 20 (a higher level would keep 20's chance).
- **Only the Amazon's own games roll:** a crit or Penetrate made by her shows only on her game; another player sees the damage, not the effect, unless the effect prefab syncs itself.

**Vanilla behaviour overridden (Harmony patch targets):**
- `Character.Damage`: prefix at `Priority.VeryHigh` (Critical Strike, Penetrate's marker on the attacker's game; the area radius on the creature's game)
- `Character.RPC_Damage`: prefix at `Priority.First` (takes Penetrate's marker off; Dodge, Avoid and Evade: can skip the original) + finalizer
- `HitData.ApplyResistance`: prefix (Penetrate: changes the modifiers argument for a marked hit only)
- `Attack.DoAreaAttack`: prefix + finalizer (record only)
- `Projectile.OnHit`: prefix + finalizer (record only)

## Amazon skill bar and bow skills

*Orlog (with CraftingOverhaul's classes UI for the slots and the cross). Built 2026-11-11; tested in-game 2026-11-15 (single player: the slots and keys, the skill button, Multishot, Freezing Arrow, Inner Sight, quick cast; two players untested), and its bow list changed the same day in the skill tree overhaul ([#66](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/66)). Roadmap task C5; branch `claude/lucid-albattani-imrei7`, merged in PR #64. Krogon asked on 2026-11-11 to "continue building out the Amazon with her other skills".*

**Controls (characters with a class; keyboard and mouse):**
- **Skill slots:** drag a learned skill from the skills panel onto one of the four slots (or `orlog_slot <1-4> <skill id>`). Passives don't take a slot; skills that don't act yet (Jab, Impale and Poison Javelin) are refused ("Doesn't work yet"). Gungnir's Throw, a basic skill, goes in a slot without points (2026-11-28, `Gungnir's Throw and the javelin skills` below). Saved per character (`orlog_slots`).
- **Slot keys F1–F4** (D2's skill keys; not used by Valheim alone or the group's mods, read from their code 2026-11-11: EquipmentAndQuickSlots Z/V/B and Left Alt, AdventureBackpacks I/Y/L, PlantEasily F6/F8/F10, arrows, Right Ctrl). A skill that needs a weapon goes on the skill button for that weapon (its slot lights up).
- **Quick cast per skill (Krogon, 2026-11-13):** a skill that needs no weapon (Inner Sight) casts straight from its slot key while quick cast is on (the default); with it off, its key puts it on the skill button for the weapon in hand and right click casts it. **Right-click a slot on the skill cross** while the inventory or skills panel is open to toggle it; a quick-cast slot has two gold sparks with fading trails running round its edge (like Dota 2's autocast). Saved per character (`orlog_quickcast_off`, the skills with it off). Weapon skills can't quick cast ("Needs a bow: it fires from the skill button").
- **Skill button: right click** fires the chosen attack skill with the weapon in hand: hold to draw the bow, release to shoot, as with the attack button. The chosen skill is remembered per weapon kind (bow, spear; Krogon 2026-11-11). With no skill chosen, or the wrong weapon, right click does nothing ("Need bow", "Not enough eitr").
- **Block needs its own key** while the game's Block key is right click (its default): `BlockKey`, **default `Mouse4`, the mouse's back side button** ("mouse button 4"; Krogon, 2026-11-15: "put the keybind on mouse side button 4. but make sure its rebindable"), any key in the Orlog config (`None` turns it off). Setting Block to another key in the game's controls also works (that key then blocks too). Valheim's dodge stays block + jump. A config file saved before this keeps its old `BlockKey` (blank): set `Mouse4` there. Side buttons are read only through the game's own input (Unity's older input numbers them the other way round, so `Mouse4` could have meant both).
- **Eitr:** each skill shot or cast pays D2's mana as eitr, D2R's numbers (Multiple Shot 4 + 1 per level; Freezing Arrow 9 + 0.25 per level; Forked Lightning 4 + 0.25 per level, Charged Strike's; Wind Lance 8 − 0.25 per level, Guided Arrow's; Strafe 10 → 15 to start and 3 → 5 a second while it streams (since 2026-11-17's rapid fire; 11 at every level before); Immolation Arrow 6 + 0.25 per level; Venom Strike 7 + 0.5 per level, Plague Javelin's; `amazon_skill_values.csv` → `mana_cost`). The slot greys out when you can't pay.

**The skills** (numbers per skill level from `Orlog/Data/amazon_skill_values.csv`; **every skill's damage is a share of the shot's own damage** since 2026-11-16, see "Skill damage scales off the weapon" below):

| Skill | Effect |
|---|---|
| Multishot (named Multiple Shot until 2026-11-15) | 2 → 21 arrows in an even fan for one arrow, each at 75% damage |
| Freezing Arrow | The arrow hits as usual. On impact: frost damage equal to **50% → 150% of the shot's damage** (Krogon, 2026-11-16: "50% at level 1, 150% at 20"; `blast_damage_pct`; tested in-game 2026-11-17 (single player)) to every enemy within 3 m (D2's 3.3 yards), freezing for 2 s (not bosses), with the ice shards' impact effect. Until 2026-11-16: 40–50 → 310–320 D2 frost points × the damage scale (about 990 at character level 1) |
| Forked Lightning (built and tested 2026-11-16, single player: "works well it seems", but "way too much damage at level 1"; balancing is for a later session; Krogon's numbers of that day) | The arrow hits as usual. If it hit an enemy, that enemy takes one fork of lightning and forks go to the nearest 3 → 7 other enemies within 4 m of its body (measured body to body), one each: **every fork deals 25% → 100% of the shot's damage as lightning** (Krogon, 2026-11-16: "25% at level 1, 100% at 20"; `fork_damage_pct`; tested in-game 2026-11-17 (single player); every fork of one shot deals the same). The fork counts and the eitr are Diablo 2's Charged Strike's. Until 2026-11-16: 1–30 → 1–322 D2 lightning points × the damage scale per fork (341 on average at character level 1, the "way too much damage" of his play-test). An arrow that hits the ground, a tree or a friend does nothing more (the eitr is spent). Each fork shows the Dundr staff's impact on its enemy and a jagged line of light from the struck enemy |
| Wind Lance (built 2026-11-16; reworked the same day after its first play-test; **the rework tested in-game that evening, single player, all 15 steps passed**; Krogon's numbers) | A charged shot: **hold right click until the bow is fully drawn, then 1.5 more seconds** (it was 3: "the charge is LOOONG"), then release. **The bow's yellow draw circle keeps narrowing past full draw and turns pale blue; when the shot is ready it is bright and pulses**, a ring sounds and wind swirls at the bow hand. The shot leaves as **a gust of wind 1.5 m wide** (0.75 m each side of its path): 100% → 233% of the arrow's own damage (Diablo 2's Guided Arrow, D2R: +7% per level; the arrow's damage types, so a fire arrow still burns), twice a fully drawn arrow's speed, no drop, 50 m. **Every enemy the gust touches is hit once, staggered (not bosses) and pushed back** with force 100 → 200 (the Staff of Embers' push to the iron sledge's). **The ground doesn't stop it:** where it would dig in, it levels out and runs just above the ground or water, following slopes a player can stand on (38°); a steeper dive or hillside, a tree, a rock or a building stops it. **Bushes and other small plants let it through, unharmed** (since the evening of 2026-11-16; tested in-game that evening, single player: the world's vegetation that the game doesn't class as a tree and that drops only what trees drop, e.g. `Bush01`, `shrub_2`; anything that can be picked; saplings and crops still growing; the log lists them). **Released before it's ready: a normal arrow, no eitr.** A crossbow has no draw: hold right click 1.5 s (the bolt is held back, the circle runs the whole way), then release. Look: the arrow three times as large with a bright core, three strands of wind spiralling tightly round it, a corkscrew wake that hangs along its path for a moment, a ring of wind at the bow and a puff at each enemy (`docs/images/wind_lance_preview.png`) |
| Strafe (**remade 2026-11-17**: Diablo 3's Rapid Fire, Krogon's idea; the volley of 2026-11-16 is removed; **tested in-game 2026-11-17, single player, bow and crossbow**: "absolutely loving it"; two players not tried) | Hold right click: the bow draws once as usual; **at full draw, while still held, arrows stream out where you aim, 6 a second**, as normal arrows (the game's own shot at full draw: speed, accuracy, flight and drop). **Each arrow 25% of the shot's damage at skill level 1, 50% at 20**, with the arrow's own damage type (fire arrows burn). **Eitr: 10 to start (15 at level 20), then 3 a second while firing (5 at level 20).** The quiver loses **one arrow a second** (the first arrow and every sixth after it). You walk at the drawn bow's slowed pace and stamina drains as for a drawn bow (the game's own, since the bow stays drawn). Stops when you let go (no extra arrow leaves), block, open a menu, change weapon, are staggered, or run out of eitr, arrows or stamina; then nothing more until you let go. Let go before full draw: one normal arrow, no eitr. **Crossbows too (Krogon, 2026-11-17):** with the button held the crossbow's own shot is held back; once loaded it streams **3 bolts a second** at the same share and one bolt a second from the quiver; no running and the held bow's stamina drain while it fires (the bows' draw drain from the game's data, halved, less with Crossbows skill); empty when it stops, then the game reloads it. Claude's choices: each arrow's push and the skill it trains shrink with its share; the bow wears once a second (with the quiver's arrow); a crossbow's stream doesn't recoil each bolt. Table rows `mana_cost`, `eitr_per_s`, `weapon_damage_pct`, `arrows_per_s`, `crossbow_per_s`, `ammo_per_s` |
| Immolation Arrow (built 2026-11-16, tested in-game 2026-11-17 (single player); Diablo 2's skill; Krogon: "Blast 25%→100%, fire 10%→30% a second") | The arrow hits as usual. **On its first hit on anything (enemy, ground, tree) it explodes:** every enemy within 3 m takes fire damage equal to **25% → 100% of the shot's damage** (`blast_damage_pct`). Then **the ground there burns for 4 seconds** (Diablo 2's time, `burn_duration_s`): every enemy with part of its body inside the 3 m takes fire damage equal to **10% → 30% of the shot's damage each second** (`burn_damage_pct`; four hits, at 1, 2, 3 and 4 seconds). Eitr 6 + 0.25 per level (Diablo 2's; 10.75 at 20). `radius_m` 3. Not built: Diablo 2's 0.6 s casting delay |
| Venom Strike (built 2026-11-16, tested in-game 2026-11-17 (single player); Krogon's own skill; Krogon: "25% at level 1, 100% at 20") | The arrow hits as usual; where it first lands **a poison cloud reaching 3 m hangs for 4 seconds.** Everything in it is poisoned for **25% → 100% of the shot's damage** (`cloud_damage_pct`; dealt over time by the game's own poison, like a poison arrow), at once and again each second it stays (hits at 0, 1, 2 and 3 seconds). Eitr 7 + 0.5 per level (16.5 at 20; Krogon: "7, +0.5 per level", Diablo 2's Plague Javelin's mana). `cloud_duration_s` 4, `radius_m` 3 |
| Snipe (built 2026-11-18, **tested in-game the same day, single player**; Krogon's own skill and numbers, decisions 2–10 and 18 of `docs/handoffs/claude-amazon-snipe-shackle-shot-m67buh.md`) | A charged shot: **hold right click past full draw; the charge fills over 3.5 s at skill level 1, 1.5 s at 20** (even steps), shown on the bow's draw circle as for Wind Lance (it keeps narrowing and turns pale blue; full: it pulses and the perfect-block ring sounds, no wind). Release any time: **the damage climbs on a square curve from a normal arrow (100%) at no charge to 150% of the shot's damage at level 1, 300% at 20, at full charge** (half a charge gives a quarter of the extra: 112% at level 1), and **the arrow flies faster with the charge, up to twice an arrow's speed** (Wind Lance's), so it drops less and reaches further; still the game's own arrow and drop. **The view zooms in while charging, evenly up to 3× at full charge** (the game's 65° view to about 24°, with the game's own smooth camera zoom, `GameCamera.SetTempFOV`), and springs back when you shoot or let go. **Stamina drains at the bow's drawing rate while charging** (most bows 14 a second, less with Bows skill): the game halves a fully drawn bow's drain and Snipe pays the other half on top until the charge is full. Out of stamina the game drops the draw, as for any bow: nothing is shot, no eitr. **Eitr: 9, +0.25 per level** (Diablo 2's Freezing Arrow's mana), **paid on any charge**; let go at full draw with no charge at all: a normal arrow, no eitr. **Crossbows too:** hold right click on a loaded crossbow; the bolt is held back while the charge, zoom and stamina drain build (the bows' drawing rate, as Strafe's crossbow stream), and fires on release. Each shot writes a line to the log ("Snipe fired (rank N): charge …%, …% of the shot's damage …"). Claude's choices: the speed rises evenly with the charge (not on the curve); the extra stamina stops once the charge is full; the mouse's sensitivity isn't changed while zoomed. Table rows `mana_cost`, `weapon_damage_pct` (at full charge), `charge_s`, `zoom_x`, `speed_pct` |
| Shackle Shot (built 2026-11-18, **tested in-game the same day, single player**; Dota 2's Windranger's Shackleshot, Krogon's numbers, decisions 11–18) | The arrow hits for **100% of the shot's damage at skill level 1, 150% at 20**. If it struck a living enemy, the mod looks **behind it, up to 8 m** (the game's harpoon line, `Harpooned`'s break distance), **within 23° of the line from you through it** (Dota's angle), for **the nearest other enemy or standing tree** (anything the game counts as a tree, `TreeBase`: not stumps or logs; a tie goes to the enemy). Found: **both are shackled and stunned for 1.75 s at level 1, 4 s at 20** (Dota's 1.75 s and 3.25 s, with its +0.75 s talent at 20); nothing behind: **the struck enemy alone, 0.6 s** (Dota's). **Stunned: no walking, swimming, flying or turning, and no new attacks** (an attack already swinging finishes). **Bosses are only slowed** (−50%, the game's Tarred slow), for the same time; the other end is held as usual. **Look (Claude's first version, no new art):** a rope between the two (the harpoon line's material where the game has it, else a plain brown line) with a little slack, and gold swirls circling above each stunned head. **Eitr: 9, +0.25 per level** (Freezing Arrow's mana); no cooldown. **Crossbows too**, as a normal crossbow shot. Each shackle writes a line to the log (who to what, for how long, how many enemies and trees were near). Table rows `mana_cost`, `weapon_damage_pct`, `shackle_s`, `alone_s`, `reach_m`, `cone_deg` |
| Inner Sight (cast) | Every enemy within 16.5 m (D2's 18 yards) is marked for 8 → 84 s and takes 5% more damage at level 1, +1% per level (24% at 20, no cap for later +skills; Krogon 2026-11-15; `InnerSightDamageBase`, `InnerSightDamagePerLevel`; the % travels to the creature owner's game as the mark's item level); you see the marked creatures glow pale blue |

**Removed by the skill tree overhaul (Krogon, 2026-11-15; [#66](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/66)):** Magic Arrow, Fire Arrow, Cold Arrow, Ice Arrow and Exploding Arrow, with their code and patches (the last commit with them is `6090e71`; Exploding Arrow's fire blast, tested on a troll, is the start for Immolation Arrow). The bow tab's other seven skills (as of 2026-11-15) come in later rounds ([#70](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/70)): **Forked Lightning and Wind Lance were built on 2026-11-16**, and **Strafe, Immolation Arrow and Venom Strike the same day** (table above); only Snipe and Shackle Shot still say "Coming later".
- **Skill damage scales off the weapon (Krogon, 2026-11-16, [#72](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/72): "All scale off the weapon"; built the same day, tested in-game 2026-11-17 (single player): all 12 steps passed).** Every damage skill deals a percentage of **the shot's own damage**, growing evenly with skill level: Multishot 75% per arrow and Wind Lance 100% → 233% (both as before), Freezing Arrow's blast 50% → 150% and each of Forked Lightning's forks 25% → 100% (new; whole percentages per level in `amazon_skill_values.csv`, rows `blast_damage_pct` and `fork_damage_pct`). "The shot's own damage" is what the game worked out for that arrow as it left the bow: bow + arrow, every damage type added up, after the draw (a half-drawn shot gives half-strength forks), your Bows skill, Dexterity, and anything else that changes the bow's damage (upgrades, Corruption's affixes); the blast and the forks then deal that share as frost or lightning, and the enemy's own resistances apply. Example, a crude bow and a wood arrow at full draw (22 + 22 = 44, game dump): a level-1 fork is 11 lightning and a level-1 blast 22 frost; at skill level 20, 44 and 66. **Added 2026-11-16 (Krogon's numbers; tested in-game 2026-11-17 (single player)):** Strafe 75% per arrow; Immolation Arrow's blast 25% → 100% and its burning 10% → 30% a second; Venom Strike's poison 25% → 100% (same crude-bow example: a Strafe arrow 33; blast 11 fire at level 1, 44 at 20; burning 4.4 a second at level 1, 13 at 20; poison 11 at level 1, 44 at 20). The percentages rise evenly per skill level to whole numbers (25, 29, 33, … 100 and 10, 11, 12, … 30). The bow and spear skills still to build follow the same rule ([#70](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/70), [#41](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/41)); the spear skills' rows in the table still hold Diablo 2's points until their round sets their shares. `SkillDamageMultiplier` (admin, default 1) multiplies the blast's and the forks' share (and, since 2026-11-16, Immolation Arrow's blast and burning and Venom Strike's poison; not Strafe's 75%, which is the arrow's own damage, like Multishot's); the tooltips show the result. Each blast and each forked shot writes one line to the log with its numbers ("Orlog: Forked Lightning (level 1): the shot's damage 44, each fork 25% = 11 lightning, 4 enemies struck …"), for the play-test.
- **Removed 2026-11-16: the damage scale** (`Orlog/Data/skill_damage_scale.csv`, proposed 2026-11-11, "not 100% confident", Krogon 2026-11-15). It turned Diablo 2's fixed damage points into Valheim points by character level (the best bow and arrow of the level's biome ÷ Diablo 2's best bow by that level: ×22 at level 1, ×13 in the Swamp and Mountains, ×4–6 later). Why it failed: Diablo 2 gives Charged Strike at level 18 and Freezing Arrow at level 30, but since the skill tree overhaul any skill can be learned at level 1, where the ×22 made a fork 6 times and a blast 18 times a whole shot.
- **Tooltips:** each skill's line in the skills panel shows its Valheim numbers now and at the next level, at your character level.
- **Agreed numbers (Krogon, 2026-11-15):** Multiple Shot's fan (5° between arrows, at most 60°) stays (Exploding Arrow's agreed 3 m radius went with the skill); Inner Sight grows 1% per level from 5%. The damage scale "feels fine for now"; balancing comes once the mods are finished and the group plays from the start (the scale was replaced on 2026-11-16, above).

**How it works:** the skill button feeds the game's own controls (`Player.SetControls`), so bows draw and shoot as usual; the attack it starts is a skill attack (`Humanoid.StartAttack`) and pays its eitr. The arrows it fires are changed as they're made (`Projectile.Setup`) and tagged; Multiple Shot sets the burst's arrow count (`Attack.FireProjectileBurst`). Impacts run on the shooter's game, which runs its arrows: Freezing Arrow's burst hits every enemy in range with our own frost hit (the area radius marked), which carries the freeze. Status effects (freeze, Inner Sight's mark) go to the creature owner's game through the game's own message; their duration travels as the hit's skill level. Inner Sight's extra damage is applied there (`Character.RPC_Damage`).

**Forked Lightning and Wind Lance (2026-11-16):** Forked Lightning runs after the arrow's own hit (`Projectile.OnHit` postfix), on the shooter's game: our own lightning hits on the struck enemy and the chosen others (`ActiveSkillRules.ForkTargets`: the nearest within reach, one fork each).

Wind Lance's charge is counted by the skill button (`Player.SetControls`): for a bow, the seconds held while the game reports a full draw (it starts again if the draw is lost); a crossbow's shot is held back until the release. `Humanoid.StartAttack` makes the shot a lance only if the charge was ready, and only then pays eitr. The charge shows on the game's own draw circle (`Hud.UpdateCrosshair` postfix: scale and colour of `Hud.m_crosshairBow`). **The lance flies itself** (`Orlog/Content/WindLance.cs`, on the shooter's game): as the shot's arrow is made (`Projectile.Setup`) its damage and push are changed, its speed multiplied, and its `Projectile` component switched off, so the game's flight, fall and hit test stop; a component of ours moves it each physics step. Per step: the ground or water under the next spot decides whether it flies free, runs along the ground or strikes it (`ActiveSkillRules.LanceGroundStep`, unit-tested); a ray along its centre line finds the first solid thing that isn't a creature (and, while it runs along the ground, isn't the ground), which stops it through the game's own arrow hit; a sphere sweep of its half width finds the enemies, each struck once through **the game's own `Projectile.OnHit`** with the game's "only stop on terrain" switch on (so the arrow carries on, and Corruption's and our own hit patches see an ordinary bow hit), then staggered (`Character.Stagger`). At its range, or when stopped, the arrow is removed. The first build (the same morning) left the flight to the game and skipped its hit on enemies with a `Projectile.OnHit` prefix: aimed down at a small creature, the arrow-thin straight line struck the ground a step behind it and never reached a second enemy; that prefix is gone. The look (`Orlog/Content/WindLanceLook.cs`, shapes in `Orlog/Rules/WindSwirlRules.cs`, preview by `tools/preview_wind_lance.py`) is drawn with line renderers and lights only, on the shooter's game: no new art.

**Strafe, Immolation Arrow and Venom Strike (2026-11-16):** no Harmony patch is added, changed in target or removed; the bodies of three existing patches were extended. `Attack.FireProjectileBurst` (prefix + finalizer) now also sets Strafe's arrow count and picks its targets (`ActiveSkillRules.StrafeTargets`, unit-tested: the enemies in view within reach, nearest the aim first, spare arrows shared out in turn). `Projectile.Setup` (prefix) gives each Strafe arrow its damage and aims it at its enemy with no drop. `Projectile.OnHit` (postfix) runs Immolation Arrow's blast and starts its burning ground, and starts Venom Strike's cloud. The areas are a small object of our own on the shooter's game (`Orlog/Content/GroundArea.cs`, not networked; the hit times come from `ActiveSkillRules.AreaTicks`, unit-tested): other players see the vanilla effects and the damage, not the ring and glow. **Look (no new art; Claude's first version, for Krogon to judge):** the burning ground and the poison cloud show a thin ring on the ground at their edge and a glow for the 4 seconds (orange for fire, green for poison), and show a vanilla impact effect (since the review, 2026-11-17: the cloud bursts with the ooze bomb's, or a poison arrow's; after that each hit shows a poison arrow's or a fire arrow's small impact; the blast keeps the fire staff's; the log says once which was found, or that none was). Each Strafe volley, each blast and each area writes one line to the log with its numbers, for the play-test. Files: `Orlog/Content/BowSkills.cs`, `Orlog/Content/GroundArea.cs` (new), `Orlog/Rules/ActiveSkillRules.cs`, `Orlog/Data/amazon_skills.csv`, `Orlog/Data/amazon_skill_values.csv`, `tools/make_orlog_tables.py`.


**Strafe as rapid fire (2026-11-17):** written with the game's code open (1.0.14, decompiled: `Player.UpdateAttackBowDraw`, `Humanoid.StartAttack`, `Attack`). While the attack button is held the game keeps the bow drawn and only shoots on the release, so the stream runs from the skill button's `Player.SetControls` prefix (once per physics step, before the game reads the controls): at full draw `Strafe.Begin` pays the start cost and clones the bow's attack; each step pays the drain and fires the arrows due (`ActiveSkillRules.StreamDue`). An arrow that takes from the quiver (`StreamTakesAmmo`) goes through `Attack.StartWithoutAnimation` (the first) or `Attack.OnAttackTrigger` (the game's ammo, bow wear, release sound and shot); the others through `Attack.FireProjectileBurst` with the bow's release effect. `BowSkills` cuts each to its share in `Projectile.Setup`. Letting go after a stream sets the draw time to -1 (the game then waits for the release without shooting) and lowers the drawing pose; the new `Humanoid.StartAttack` prefix keeps any game shot back meanwhile. A crossbow: the skill button holds its shot back; the stream starts when it is loaded, fires the bolts that take from the quiver through `Attack.UseAmmo` and `ProjectileAttackTriggered` (not `OnAttackTrigger`, which unloads it), turns the controls' run off (the `Player.SetControls` prefix now also takes `run`) and drains the held bow's stamina; at the end `ResetLoadedWeapon` makes the game reload. New file `Orlog/Content/Strafe.cs`.
**Snipe and Shackle Shot (2026-11-18):** written with the game's code open (1.0.14, decompiled: `GameCamera.SetTempFOV`/`UpdateFOV`, `Player.UpdateAttackBowDraw`, `ItemData.GetDrawStaminaDrain`, `Character.CanMove`, `Humanoid.StartAttack`, `SEMan.AddStatusEffect`, `SE_Harpooned`, `LineConnect`). **Snipe** is a second charged skill for the skill button (`ActiveSkillRules.Charges`): the `Player.SetControls` prefix counts its charge as Wind Lance's (from full draw; a crossbow's bolt held back until the release) over `charge_s` instead of `hold_s`, pays the extra stamina each physics step (`Snipe.ChargeStep`, with Strafe's held-bow drain, which now uses a bow's own draw drain and a crossbow the bows'), and `Humanoid.StartAttack`'s postfix fires it on any charge (`ActiveSkillRules.ChargeFires`), keeping how full the charge was; `Projectile.Setup` then sets the arrow's damage (`SnipeDamagePercent`, the square curve) and speed (`SnipeSpeedPercent`). The zoom is no patch: each frame (`OrlogPlugin.Update` → `Snipe.Tick`) the camera's target view follows the charge (`ActiveSkillRules.ZoomFov`), and goes back to the game's own base view (`GameCamera.m_fovBase`, 65°) when it ends. **Shackle Shot:** `Projectile.OnHit`'s postfix finds the anchor (`ActiveSkillRules.ShackleAnchor`, unit-tested: the enemies of `Character.GetAllCharacters` and the `TreeBase` objects in an 8 m sphere, by their offset on the ground) and adds our stun (`Orlog_Shackled`) or the boss's slow (`Orlog_ShackleSlow`, speed −0.5) through `SEMan.AddStatusEffect`, which the game sends to whichever game runs the creature (as Inner Sight's mark); the seconds ride in the skill level (`StatusEffect.SetLevel` postfix, as the freeze's). **New patch: a `Character.CanMove` postfix** (`ShackleShot.Stunned`) answers false for a creature (not a player) with the stun; the game's own code then stops its walking and flying (`UpdateWalking`, `UpdateFlying`), its turning and its new attacks (`Humanoid.StartAttack` refuses while it can't move). That is also why the stun stops fliers, which the game's Immobilized (speed −1000, Freezing Arrow's freeze) doesn't: `UpdateFlying` ignores speed modifiers. Swimming (`UpdateSwimming`) doesn't check `CanMove` but reads status-effect speed, so the stun also carries Immobilized's speed −1000 and no jumping. The rope and swirls (`ShackleShot.ShackleLook`) are drawn on the shooter's game only. New files `Orlog/Content/Snipe.cs`, `Orlog/Content/ShackleShot.cs`.
**Known issues / not yet:**
- **Wind Lance's rework passed Krogon's play-test (2026-11-16, single player, Valheim 1.0.21, build `9fc528b`, 15 steps, no notes but "yes" to the 1.5 s hold):** the charge and its circle, the look, through enemies and along the ground, what stops it, the sky, a troll, rank 20, fire arrows, a crossbow, the older skills, no errors. From his log (13 lances, all rank 20): the wind is drawn with `Sprites/Default` (the arrow carries no trail material); a bow's lance flies at 100 m/s and a crossbow's at 400 m/s; one lance ended at a bush (`Bush01`) after 32 m and one at a rock; **two ended at once at the ground ("flew 0 m … ended at Terrain") after striking the 1–2 enemies beside him: aimed down more steeply than 38° at close enemies, the lance strikes the ground, as built; Krogon keeps that ("Leave it as it is").** Bushes: "Pass through bushes", built the same evening and **passed his check** (build `00c7b57`: a lance through a bush flew its 50 m; a rock and a birch still stopped it). The log's list of what it passes through: `BlueberryBush`, `Bush01`, `Bush01_deepnorth`, `Bush01_heath`, `Crow`, `Greydwarf_Root`, `HugeRoot1`, `LingonberryBush`, `RaspberryBush`, `shrub_2`, `shrub_2_heath`. **Three of those aren't small plants** (a crow, and two kinds of large root, which also drop only wood): the gust goes through them too, harmlessly; left as it is until Krogon says otherwise. Young thin trees (`Beech_small1`, `FirTree_small`) and stumps still stop it: the game classes them as trees. Not tried: anything with two players. Each lance writes two lines to the log ("Wind Lance fired …", "Wind Lance flew N m …, struck K enemies, ended at …") and an early release one ("released before its charge was ready"), for the play-test. Claude's own choices, open to change: the gust runs 0.75 m above the ground (its half width, so it reaches from the dirt to about head height); it follows slopes up to the 38° a player can stand on; it skips creatures that are neutral until attacked (the game's own arrows hit them); the look's sizes and colours; the circle narrowing to 0.06 and its pale blue; a crossbow charges with no stamina cost; the bow skill rises with each enemy struck (the game's own rule for an arrow hit). Other players see the lance as a fast arrow without the wind.
- **Forked Lightning's balance (Krogon, 2026-11-16): "way too much damage at level 1"** (five 4-star greylings almost killed by one arrow). It followed the damage scale (×22 at character level 1). **Remade the same day ([#72](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/72)): forks are a share of the shot's damage (above); **passed his play-test on 2026-11-17** (`docs/playtests/claude-magical-babbage-slwwpn.md`: "testing has passed"; all 12 steps Pass on the phone page, no notes), with its last two steps (a troll with greylings; rank 20 with nine brutes). Not checked in the game's code this round (no copy to read): that the damage the game hands over as the arrow is made already includes the draw and the Bows skill; the new log lines show the numbers. Claude's choice, open to change: the struck enemy's fork is on top of the 3 → 7 forks to others. Other players see the forks as bursts without the lines.
- **After the code review of the three new skills (a second model read the code, 2026-11-17; fixed the same day, before Krogon's play-test, which passed):** Strafe no longer picks an enemy that is on screen but behind the bow (the camera sits behind the player); the burning ground now lies on the ground, floor or rock straight under the hit (an arrow in a troll's chest left it 5 m up), while the blast stays at the hit and the poison cloud stays where the arrow landed; the burning ground no longer replays the fire blast's explosion every second (the blast plays it once; each later hit shows a fire arrow's small impact; the cloud bursts with the ooze bomb's effect, then a poison arrow's small impact each second); a Strafe volley that couldn't be planned stays one normal arrow at full damage. **Left as it is, to judge in the play-test:** (1) the game's own poison doesn't add up: a new dose only replaces a smaller one, so an enemy that stands in the cloud all 4 seconds takes roughly one and a half doses, not four ("re-poisoned each second it stays" means kept topped up); (2) the game deals fire as burning over about 5 seconds, as a fire arrow does, so the blast and the ground's hits arrive spread out, the last of it some seconds after the ground stops burning; (3) Corruption's on-hit affixes (life steal, Skadi, Rallying) and Orlog's Penetrate run on every hit of the burning ground and the cloud, with the weapon in hand at that moment, not the bow that shot (`docs/mod-compatibility.md`) (**decided 2026-11-18, Krogon: "Blast counts, ticks don't"; built and tested in-game the same day, single player: the ticks are typed as burning and poison and trigger no on-hit affix; Penetrate still applies; the blast counts as the bow's**); (4) against one enemy, or with none in view, every arrow of a Strafe volley flies the same way: at skill level 20 that is seven arrows at 75% each on one target (Diablo 2's rule); (5) an arrow that lands in water starts its blast or cloud at the surface.
- **Strafe, Immolation Arrow and Venom Strike (built 2026-11-16, [#70](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/70); **tested in-game 2026-11-17, single player: all 14 steps passed, no notes**; limits):** written without the game's code open; nothing tried with two players (`PLAYTEST.md` → Session 2, 2j step 8). (Strafe's volley, tested here, was replaced by rapid fire on 2026-11-17; see its row above.) Diablo 2's 0.6 s casting delay on Immolation Arrow is not built (the skills have no cooldowns). The burning ground and the cloud sit where the arrow hit and don't follow slopes. The area's hits don't train the Bows skill. The area ends early if the shooter dies. Whether Corruption's on-hit affixes (life steal) react to every area hit is to watch in the play-test. The look is a first version (he passed the verdict step without a note). Play-test: `docs/playtests/claude-magical-babbage-slwwpn.md`, second round.
- **Untested in-game.** Read in the cloud's copy of the game code (1.0.14); the input path (`SetControls` parameter names) is the riskiest part on 1.0.21.
- **No gamepad support** for the skill button and slot keys yet; with a gamepad nothing changes.
- **Snipe and Shackle Shot (built 2026-11-18; tested in-game the same day, single player: all 22 steps passed, no notes, "all playtesting is done and passed"; nothing tried with two players: `PLAYTEST.md` → Session 2, 2j step 9).** Limits: Shackle Shot's rope and swirls show on the shooter's game only (other players see the creatures stand still); a creature shackled again while still stunned takes the new time (a 0.6 s lone shackle can cut a 4 s one short); the anchor is searched on the ground's plane (a creature on a ledge above counts); a tree is the trunk's position, so a wide crown doesn't count; only standing trees, not stumps, logs or large roots. Snipe: any release after full draw pays (decision 6), so a quick tap at full draw already costs its eitr for a little more than a normal arrow; Wind Lance's and Snipe's draw circle look the same. Play-test: `docs/playtests/claude-amazon-snipe-shackle-shot-m67buh.md`.
- **The freeze stops movement only** (the game's Immobilized: speed −1000); a frozen creature may still swing.
- **Glow only on the caster's game;** other players see no glow (the mark and extra damage work for everyone's hits).
- No cooldowns (D2's bow skills and Inner Sight have none). Gungnir's Throw, Lightning Bolt and Plague Javelin: `Gungnir's Throw and the javelin skills` below (2026-11-28); the other spear skills later.
- **Fixed and tested (Krogon, 2026-11-15, single player, a troll): a direct hit on a big creature made no burst on it.** Cause, found with temporary log lines (removed since): the game's `Character.GetCharactersInRange` measures to a creature's feet, so a blast on a troll's chest (about 5 m up) found nothing within 3 m; greylings, being small, were found. Now the blast reaches any creature with part of its body (its collider) within the radius, and always the creature the arrow struck.
- **Fixed (2026-11-15, to re-test): after a respec the skill cross kept the old skills** (visible only; their keys did nothing). A respec now empties the slots and the skill button, and a slot whose skill has no points reads as empty (which also cleans characters respecced before the fix). Quick cast choices are kept.
- **Fixed (2026-11-15, to re-test): F2 also opened the game's network-stats panel.** While F2 is a slot key and the character has a class, plain F2 is the skill's; **Ctrl+F2** opens the panel. With Ctrl held, F1–F4 don't use a slot (Ctrl+F1 and Ctrl+F3 stay the game's mouse capture and hide-the-HUD).
- **Fixed and tested (Krogon, 2026-11-15, single player):** Inner Sight also fired the Forsaken power. Cause (game code): the "gpower" gesture's animation event (`CharacterAnimEvent.GPower`) calls `Player.ActivateGuardianPower`. Now, for 3 seconds after a cast, that call is skipped once (the log says "Inner Sight's gesture didn't trigger the Forsaken power"); pressing the power key yourself (`Player.StartGuardianPower`) still works at once.
- **Found in the code review (2026-11-11), to watch in the play-test:** running out of eitr in the middle of a skill draw now keeps the draw and shoots a normal arrow on release (fixed the same day); Freezing Arrow hits count for Corruption's life steal of the bow, once per creature caught in the blast.

**Vanilla behaviour overridden (Harmony patch targets):**
- `Player.SetControls`: prefix (the skill button holds attack; block from `BlockKey`; since 2026-11-16 it also counts Wind Lance's charge and holds a crossbow's charged shot back until the release; since 2026-11-18 Snipe's charge and its extra stamina)
- `Hud.UpdateCrosshair`: postfix (2026-11-16: while a charged shot is held, sets the scale and colour of the game's bow draw circle, `Hud.m_crosshairBow`; shows it for a crossbow; Snipe's charge too since 2026-11-18)
- `Humanoid.StartAttack`: postfix (marks the skill attack, pays eitr; a charged skill released early stays a normal shot (Snipe: only with no charge at all, 2026-11-18, and the charge reached is kept for its arrow); Strafe let go before full draw too); **prefix since 2026-11-17** (`Strafe.HoldRelease`: while a Strafe stream runs or has stopped during the same hold, the local player's attack doesn't start, so the game's own release shot is held back; skips the original only then)
- `Attack.FireProjectileBurst`: prefix + finalizer (Multiple Shot's arrow count; Strafe's volley count and targets 2026-11-16 to 2026-11-17, removed)
- `Projectile.Setup`: prefix (skill damage, fan direction, tag; since 2026-11-18 Snipe's damage and speed from its charge and Shackle Shot's arrow damage; for a Wind Lance: its damage, push and speed, and **the arrow's `Projectile` component is switched off** so our own flight moves it; since 2026-11-16 also Strafe's arrow damage, and from 2026-11-16 to 2026-11-17 its aim at its enemy with no drop; since the rapid fire, each streamed arrow's share of the shot)
- `Projectile.OnHit`: postfix (Freezing Arrow's burst; Forked Lightning's forks; since 2026-11-16 Immolation Arrow's blast and burning ground and Venom Strike's cloud; since 2026-11-18 Shackle Shot's shackle). Not patched for Wind Lance, but **called by it directly** for each enemy struck and for what stops it (the prefix that could skip the game's hit, added the morning of 2026-11-16, was removed the same day)
- `Player.StartGuardianPower`: postfix (record only: the player started their power)
- `Player.ActivateGuardianPower`: prefix, can skip (once, within 3 s of Inner Sight's gesture)
- `ConnectPanel.Update`: prefix + postfix (record only, then undoes the panel's F2 toggle when F2 is a slot key and Ctrl isn't held). Since 2026-11-26 the panel's `m_root` is read by name: Valheim 1.0.30 removed it, which made the old patch throw every frame. Without it F2 opens the panel as well as using the slot (one warning in the log)
- `Character.RPC_Damage`: prefix (Inner Sight's extra damage on a marked creature)
- `StatusEffect.SetLevel`: postfix (the freeze's and Inner Sight's duration; since 2026-11-18 also Shackle Shot's stun and boss slow)
- `Character.CanMove`: postfix (**new 2026-11-18**, Shackle Shot's stun: false for a creature, not a player, carrying `Orlog_Shackled`; the game then stops its moving, turning and new attacks)
- Status effects added through Jötunn: `Orlog_Freeze` (copy of `Immobilized`), `Orlog_InnerSight`, `Orlog_Shackled`, `Orlog_ShackleSlow` (2026-11-18, Shackle Shot; our own `SE_Stats`)
- Removed 2026-11-15 with the skills they served: `Attack.HaveAmmo`, `Attack.EquipAmmoItem`, `Attack.UseAmmo` (Magic Arrow), `Character.Damage` prefix (Ice Arrow), `Projectile.OnHit` prefix and finalizer

## Gungnir's Throw and the javelin skills

*Orlog (and CraftingOverhaul's skills panel for the Basic card). Built 2026-11-28 on `claude/optimistic-ramanujan-acdyf8` ([#41](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/41), roadmap C6) with Krogon's decisions of that day (the branch's handoff, decisions 1–18; look approved from `docs/images/javelins_preview.png`). **Tested in-game 2026-11-28 (single player): 12 of 18 passed, then the fixes' re-test passed (8 of 8). Two players untested.***

**Gungnir's Throw (a basic skill):** every character with a class has it, at level 1, for no skill points. It shows as a small **Basic** card above the three skill tabs in the skills panel ("Basic · always learned"); drag it onto a slot like any other skill. With a spear in hand and Gungnir's Throw on the skill button, **right click throws a gold ghost copy of the spear where you aim; the spear never leaves your hand**. Hold right click to keep throwing, as the game's own throw does. It costs the throw's stamina (flint spear 8, Nord spear 18: the game's `secondary_attack_stamina`), deals the throw's damage, raises the Spears skill on a hit, and costs no eitr. The game's own throw (middle mouse) is unchanged. The Abyssal Harpoon throws with its left-click attack (it has no other throw), so for it the skill uses that attack; its ghost doesn't pull and has no rope.

**The ghost spear's look** (on every player's game: each spear's throw projectile is copied three times as networked prefabs, `Orlog_GhostSpear_*`, `Orlog_BoltSpear_*`, `Orlog_PlagueSpear_*`): the spear's own model drawn see-through warm gold, a soft gold light and a faint trail; it sticks where it lands (ground or enemy), then fades out over about a second. Nothing can be picked up.

**Lightning Bolt** (javelin tab): the ghost spear wrapped in crackling blue-white lightning, **twice as fast** as a thrown spear (`speed_pct` 200), stopping at the first enemy it hits; it deals **75% → 200%** of the throw's damage (even steps), **all of it lightning**, and bursts with the lightning staff's hit effect. Eitr: Diablo 2's 6 + 0.25 per level (6 → 10.75), plus the throw's stamina.

**Plague Javelin** (javelin tab): the ghost spear drips green in flight and hits for **100%** of the throw's damage; where it lands a **poison cloud spreads** evenly from nothing out to **4 m** over **3 s at level 1, 6.8 s at 20** (Diablo 2 Resurrected's poison duration). Each second everything inside is poisoned (the game's own poison) for **25% → 100%** of the throw's damage (Venom Strike's share; scaled by `SkillDamageMultiplier`). Its look on the ground: the ooze burst, then soft green puffs that fill the cloud's reach as it spreads (Krogon, 2026-11-28: "it would be better if the poison cloud grew in size"), with a thin, half see-through ring at its edge and a dim green light (Venom Strike's ring and light, toned down; the ring's design for all area skills is [#105](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/105)). Eitr: Diablo 2's 7 + 0.5 per level (7 → 16.5), plus the throw's stamina.

**Rules:**
- The throw is the spear's own secondary attack, copied by the game for that one throw: the mod swaps in the ghost projectile, stops the spear being used up, and for Lightning Bolt doubles its speed. If eitr runs short as the throw starts, it flies as a plain Gungnir's Throw (no eitr) rather than drop the spear.
- The hit counts as a spear hit: Corruption's on-hit affixes of the spear apply to it; the cloud's ticks are poison over time and trigger none (decision 19 of the bow round).
- **Multiplayer (read in code, not tested):** the thrower's game runs the ghost and its hit, as the game does for thrown spears; the ghost prefabs exist on every game (every player has Orlog), so others see the gold ghost and its fade; the cloud and its ring are on the thrower's game only, like Venom Strike's, its poison applied through the game's own damage message.

**Known issues and limits:**
- **Fixed after the first play-test (2026-11-28), re-test passed:** the ghost copies were never made: they were built from `ObjectDB`, whose item list is still empty when the game's prefabs first load, so every throw flew as the game's own spear (no gold, glow or trail, gone on impact). They are now built from the game's loaded prefabs (Jötunn's cache), again once the game's prefabs are registered, and on the first throw if one is missing; the log names how many. The ghost keeps its model after impact (`m_hideOnHit` cleared). The look's light, trail, sparks and drips sit on a child object, so a spear projectile's own light or trail can't block them; the first ghost logs what it got.
- **Fixed 2026-11-29 (Krogon's screenshot): the Basic card had no icon.** Gungnir's Throw now has a placeholder icon like the other skills (a gold spear with gold streaks; `tools/make_skill_icons.py`, `CraftingOverhaul/Assets/Skills/GungnirsThrow.*`).
- The ghost's material is the engine's `Sprites/Default` shader tinted gold (the one Forked Lightning's lines and the area rings use).
- The cloud poisons once a second starting 1 s after landing, so at level 20 (6.8 s) its last poisoning (6 s) reaches 3.5 m while the ring reaches 4 m at the end.
- A spear from another mod gets ghosts too if its item exists when the game's prefabs load; one that doesn't throws its own projectile (no spear dropped; logged once).
- Jab, Impale and Poison Javelin can still be learned but do nothing and can't go in a slot ("Doesn't work yet"); they come in a later round (#41 stays open).

**Vanilla behaviour overridden (Harmony patch targets):**
- `Player.SetControls`: the existing prefix now also takes `secondaryAttack` and `secondaryAttackHold`: a throw skill on the skill button holds the spear's throw instead of the attack.
- `Humanoid.StartAttack`: the existing postfix now also handles the secondary attack, for a throw skill only (the game's own throw is untouched): pays eitr and makes the throw a ghost.
- `Projectile.Setup`: **new postfix** (`Javelins.GhostDamage`): no item spawned back, Lightning Bolt's lightning, Plague Javelin's hit, no harpoon pull.
- `Projectile.OnHit`: **new postfix** (`Javelins.GhostImpact`): Plague Javelin's cloud.
- Prefabs added through Jötunn: three ghost copies of each spear's throw projectile (cloned from Jötunn's prefab cache, with their item respawn off, the model kept on impact, rope removed, `GhostSpearLook` added); the Abyssal Harpoon's main-attack projectile included.

## Boss bonus skill points

*Orlog. Built 2026-11-11; tested in-game 2026-11-15 (single player: Eikthyr's first kill gave +1 point, a second gave nothing; multiplayer untested). Roadmap task C4 (boss XP is built and tested on Eikthyr, see [Levels and XP](#levels-and-xp); the respec item is low priority, [#67](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/67)).*

**What it does:** a character's first kill of each biome's boss gives skill points, Diablo 2's quest points (12 in all; Krogon 2026-11-11): 1 each for Eikthyr, the Elder, Bonemass and Moder, 2 each for Yagluth, the Queen, Fader and Kall Fimbulbringer (`Orlog/Data/biome_levels.csv` → `boss_skill_points`). Every player within `ShareRange` of the kill gets them, like the kill's XP, once per character: recorded by biome (`orlog_boss_kills`), so Kall's three phases count once and a second Eikthyr gives nothing. A character without a class records the kill too and gets the points once it has one. "First victory over this foe: +N skill points" shows. A respec keeps them (they're earned, like level points).

Unit-tested (`OrlogBossPointTests`: 12 in all, Kall's phases as one boss, saving, adding to unspent points).

**Tested in single player (Krogon, 2026-11-15):** "yes boss points worked, second Eikthyr gave nothing." Multiplayer (both players getting the points) in `PLAYTEST.md` → 2j.

**Known issues:** kills by fall damage or another creature give no XP and so no points (as for XP).

**Vanilla behaviour overridden (Harmony patch targets):** none new (the existing `Character.OnDeath` prefix sends an `Orlog_BossKill` message).

## Classes, levels and skill trees

**Status:** Planned. Design only, no code (branch `claude/class-skills`).

Diablo 2-style classes in a separate plugin: the Amazon first (group vote, 2026-10-30), then the Barbarian, maybe the Paladin. Character levels from XP, with stat points (D2's Strength, Dexterity, Vitality, Energy) and skill points each level, spent in D2's three-tab trees (skills open by level; prerequisites only where both skills are built). Only the skills that work in Valheim; the rest are reworked or dropped. Vanilla skills keep levelling by use. EpicMMOSystem was compared and is not used as a base. Design, open questions and build order: `docs/class-design.md`; D2 reference data: `data/d2-reference/`.

**Harmony patch targets:** none yet.

Details: [Map tiers and affixes](#map-tiers-and-affixes).
  - **Added:** six map tiers, where the tier is the map's rarity and affix count (white, Magic with 1 or 2 affixes, Rare with 3 or 4, and tier 6 a tier 5 map corrupted with a shard); table `map_tiers.csv` (waves ×2 to ×4.5, guaranteed 2-stars none to all, heart shards 1–4 to 6–14, heart gear floor); `drop_modifiers.csv` `rip_tier_1` … `rip_tier_6` (×1.25 to ×2.5). Four affixes in `map_affixes.csv`: Swarming, Hardened, Rabid, Smouldering (danger only; the weather ones Frostbitten, Drowned and Choking are `later`). Orbs craft maps at the Riftforge's Forge tab as they craft gear, and a shard corrupts a full tier 5 map into tier 6 without ever breaking it (Divine, Jeweller's, Affix Splinters and Shatter are refused on maps). Maps go into the forge from the bag or the Maps tab. Map names coloured by rarity (tier 6: "Corrupted {biome} map", red), the tier as a number on the icon, a tooltip with the tier, the affixes and what the tier gives, and the tier and affixes on the rip's portals and heart hover texts. The tier and affixes travel with the rip request and are stamped on the rip's objects and every wave creature; the waves, stars, loot and affixes follow the stamp. New console cheats `co_map`, `co_rip` and `co_rip fallback`. A per-player setting `WarnBeforeCorruptingMaps` was added here and removed again the same day (no map pop-up, decision 14).
  - **Added (#84):** the explored-land fallback (unexplored land first; explored land only when none is left, with a message) and **96 m from anything a player placed for every rip**. Krogon: "96m, every rip, everything a player placed".
  - **Changed:** `BaseDistance` now means 96 m from anything a player placed (0 = 96 m) instead of a ward's radius from beds, wards and portals. `WaveMultiplier`, `ChestShardsMin` and `ChestShardsMax` are only used if `map_tiers.csv` fails to load. The heart's spill and kill loot count by the rip's tier (was always tier 1). A used map is the exact map used, not the first of its biome in the bag. Rip creatures of rips opened with this build carry the rip's closing time and leave once it has passed (fixes the old known issue that maddened creatures stay). Wave spawns skip spots inside a player base. Every game counts the wave creatures it has loaded, not only its own spawns. Rip request and reply carry the tier and affixes; the server derives the tier from the affix count. Grown crops count as player-made in the 96 m check.
  - **New and changed Harmony patches:** new `Character.RPC_Damage` prefix (Hardened), `Character.Damage` prefix at `Priority.Low` (Smouldering) and `InventoryGrid.UpdateGui` postfix (the tier number on map icons); changed `ItemData.GetTooltip` postfix, `InventoryGrid.CreateItemTooltip` postfix, `ItemDrop.GetHoverText` postfix, `Humanoid.UseItem` prefix, `InventoryGui.OnRightClickItem` prefix, `Player.UpdateTeleport` postfix, `CharacterDrop.OnDeath` postfix and `Character.Awake` postfix (Maddened).
  - **Needs the new build on the server and every game** (the rip messages changed; a tiered map on an older server is kept with a message). **Version bump needed at merge (Corruption), by the coordinator.**
  - **Not built:** the three weather affixes (next round, after the 1.0.30 `co_dump`), corrupted-only affixes (later), 3-star creatures (#39).

Multiplayer left for later by Krogon (`PLAYTEST.md` → Session 2, 2g).
- **Fix (corrupted offerings, review): a corrupted summon stays corrupted and costs one item.** The game running the altar starts the summon and marks it corrupted; offering anything during the spawn delay, from anyone, changes nothing. Before, a normal or wrong item during the delay made the boss normal after the corrupted item was taken, and a second player's corrupted offering could summon a second boss. Passed alone; two players untested.
- **Fix (loot, review): saved affix values that aren't finite numbers** (NaN, infinity) make the item give no affix effects, with its saved data kept as it is; `co_affix` won't overwrite it. Finite values are never clamped. Passed alone.
- **Fix (Orlog, review): stat and skill point totals can't overflow.** A damaged save with huge counts reads as everything spent instead of throwing in the character API, `orlog_info` and the spending checks; no point is refunded or removed. Passed alone.
- **Fix (talismans, review): a provoked creature keeps fighting back when its owner logs out.** The fight-back time is kept on the creature per player in shared world time, so another player's game taking over the creature keeps it; new hits refresh it; other players and creatures are unaffected. Passed alone; the owner change needs two players.
- **Bug recorded: weapon-skill affix shows one less in the Skills tab** ([#56](https://github.com/krogon-games/JotunnModStub/issues/56), from the 2026-10-31 play-test). - **Play-test (single player):** the gathering review fixes passed (Seismic and Odin's Grace on deposits and tin, tool tier on silver, Veinbreaker's resets, only your swings); multiplayer left for later.
- **Fix (gathering, review): Seismic and Odin's Grace on your own deposits.** When your game ran the copper, tin or rock you hit (playing alone, or standing nearest), a hit that broke the struck chunk found no touching chunks, so Seismic and Odin's Grace only broke that one chunk. The touching chunks are now read before the hit is sent, so they're the same whoever runs the deposit; the tool tier still applies to each, and a chunk never drops twice.
- **Fix (gathering, review): Veinbreaker's count** starts over whenever the tool is unequipped (switching items, putting it away, swimming, eating, dropping or breaking it), as AFFIX_RULES says. Before, it only started over when you mined with a different tool.
- **Change (gathering, Krogon's decision): only your swings get your tool's affixes.** A tree, log, stump or rock that breaks without a player's hit (a rolling log, a falling tree, a creature) gives normal drops. The copper deposit a breaking rock turns into still gets them.
- **Odin's Grace on trees logs each whole-tree harvest** (diagnostics for a two-player check, no behaviour change), and the new testing command `co_own` makes your game run the object you look at.
- **Play-test:** Skadi on a bow passed with the devcommands message (stacks 1–5, kept after switching weapons mid-flight, none for a plain bow). Every single-player step of the review fixes has passed; multiplayer still to test.

### 2026-11-07
- **Play-test, second run (single player):** tabs (message now fits), death-path drops, chests alone and arrow life steal passed. Skadi showed no blue numbers because the game has none (one total per hit); added a devcommands-only top-left message per Skadi hit for testing.
- **Play-test (single player):** the inventory tabs' safe loading passed (all six steps) and Magic/Rare drops from creatures whose body carries their loot passed (greydwarfs, at the expected rate). The locked-tab message ran off the screen: now three short lines (to re-check).
- **Rip waves log their counts** (diagnostics only, no behaviour change): which game runs a rip's waves, and how many wave creatures it counts before each spawn, to check a suspected undercount after the heart changes hands.
- **Hit affixes follow the item the hit came from** (review fix, untested in game): knockback, backstab, life steal and Skadi on an arrow, bolt or bomb (direct hit and splash) use the item it was shot from, not the weapon held when it lands; procs use their own item (Wrath the weapon, Reprisal the blocker).
- **Craft from chests: exclusive access before paying** (review fix, untested in game): a chest is paid from only once its owner has handed it over through the game's own chest handshake and it's marked in use; the craft's "enough materials?" check counts only those chests; taking from chests is all or nothing; a refused chest means nothing crafted and nothing taken (unless what you carry covers it). Fixes free or double-paid crafts when two players use the same chest at once.
- **Magic and Rare drops from every kill** (review fix, untested in game): creatures whose corpse carries their loot (most of them) now roll too, once per nearby player, on the creature owner's game; genuine no-drop creatures, players and tamed creatures still don't.
- **Inventory tabs load safely** (review fix, untested in game; testing command `co_tabs_damage`): a tab whose saved data can't be read completely (damaged, or an item whose prefab is missing) is locked for the session instead of shown, its saved data is kept unchanged, and a message says so. Fixes currency coming back after a reload, and pickups vanishing, when one tab was damaged.

- **Fix: shards disappeared with the new look.** Texture loading threw during registration (raw data without mip levels), so the shard item didn't exist. Textures now load with `SetPixels32`, and a failing custom look falls back to the tinted Bloodstone instead of blocking registration.
- **Corruption shard: its own model and texture.** A jagged red crystal with glowing veins replaces the Bloodstone look (generated by `tools/make_shard.py`, embedded in the DLL). Setting `ShardCustomModel`. Untested in-game (77 tests).
- **Corruption shards tested in-game:** 1-star, 2-star and boss (Eikthyr) drops work and the shard is red. Dropped shards now glow red on the ground so they're easier to spot (configurable, untested).
- **Config reload on save** (dev aid, both plugins): saving a `.cfg` file while the game runs reloads it. Off switch: `0 - Development` → `ReloadConfigOnFileChange`. Marked for removal or default-off before release.
- **Corruption shards:** 1-star creature drops confirmed in-game.
- **Talismans tested in-game (single player):** working as intended with all mods loaded after the fixes.
- **Talisman fixes after the first play-test with all mods:** charms no longer keep the base trinket's Adrenaline 50 or its "Trinket" subtitle, and charms picked up from the ground are recognised by the talisman slots. The slots themselves showed up correctly.
- **Talismans: 2 talisman slots plus the vanilla trinket.** Talismans are no longer trinkets; they work from their own slots (default 2, admin setting `Slots`), added to EquipmentAndQuickSlots' equipment panel through its API, and are published to other players through the player object. Without EquipmentAndQuickSlots they do nothing. Untested in-game (74 tests).
- **Mod compatibility** (`docs/mod-compatibility.md`): checked EquipmentAndQuickSlots 3.1.3, PlantEasily 2.3.1 and AdventureBackpacks 2.4.6 against our patches. Fixed in craft-from-chests: with AdventureBackpacks' craft-from-backpack, a cost the backpack covered was charged to nearby chests too; and the backpack's hidden container could be counted as a chest. Untested in-game.
- Affix numbers for the new affixes approved. Caps moved into `Corruption/Data/affix_caps.csv` with a validator (`Corruption/Rules/AffixCaps.cs`); movement-speed cap raised to 35% (71 tests). No gameplay change yet.
- Affix table: added Magnetic, Charring, Fleet, Foraging, Bloodforged, Deathless, Shieldwall, Rallying, Skald's, Steadfast, Reprisal, Wrath, Skadi and Fenrir (79 affixes); new `requires` and `lower_is_better` columns and an `any` pool; name clashes fixed and now rejected by the validator (68 tests). Not used in game yet; no gameplay change.

### 2026-10-27
- Drop tables approved: stars and rip tier 1 now raise Magic and Rare chances too; proposed rip, scar and terror-zone values accepted. If a kill rolls both Magic and Rare, only the Rare drops (`DropTable.Roll`, 67 tests). No gameplay change yet.
- Added the drop chance tables (`Corruption/Data/drop_chances.csv`, `drop_modifiers.csv`) with a reader and chance calculation (`Corruption/Rules/DropTable.cs`) and tests (65 tests), plus a guide to the data folder. Not used in game yet; no gameplay change.
- Added `Corruption/Data/affixes.csv`, the single table of every affix (gathering and gear), with a validator (`Corruption/Rules/AffixTable.cs`) and tests that check the real file (60 tests). Not used in game yet; no gameplay change.
- Added handover docs: `docs/CURRENT_STATE.md`, `DECISIONS.md`, `ARCHITECTURE.md`, `GAME_DESIGN.md`, and a rewritten `CLAUDE.md` with an update routine.
- Decided: our own loot system, not Epic Loot.
- Gathering tool affixes specified (universal, mining and woodcutting pools for pickaxes and one-handed woodcutting axes) in `docs/AFFIX_RULES.md`, with eligible tools from the game data and how each affix maps onto the game code. Loot rules agreed: crafting makes vanilla items, orbs upgrade them, monsters drop rolled items; Magic 1+1, Rare 2+2 affixes; tool affix ranges are the same for every tool. Then: Magic 1 in 50 and Rare 1 in 100 monster drops (tunable); the same-range rule extended to all gear; Odin's Grace made a whole-tree / touching-chunks harvest; Jotun Bane, Thunderblood and Frostfire axes eligible; seven low-impact affixes approved for variety; every one-handed axe rolls both gathering and weapon affixes; flat-value affixes become a % of the item's stat where possible, with one-range numbers proposed for general gear. Not built (loot system, phase 7).
- Drafted the gear affix pool (prefixes, suffixes, biome tiers, twin resonance, range display), now in `docs/AFFIX_RULES.md`.
- Saved the storage and quest systems brief (`docs/storage-quests-design.md`) for later.
- No mod code changed.

### 2026-10-26
- **Talismans (design phase 2):** one charm per creature family, worked out from game data. It repels that family from the wearer unless provoked. The family rules and exclusions are unit-tested against the real 1.0 creature list (53 tests in total). Not yet tested in-game.
- Fixed the raven repeatedly returning to the Riftforge: its replacement text kept the game's "always show" default.
- Fixed constant `IndexOutOfRangeException` errors from the build menu: the Riftforge recipe had 6 ingredients, and building pieces support at most 5. Leather scraps removed.
- **Riftforge tested in-game.** The raven now gives Riftforge/Ginnungagap lore instead of the Galdr table's, and particle effects and lights are tinted too, so the glow turns red rather than pink.
- The Riftforge is now red to match the shard (was purple); its colour and strength are configurable.
- **Corruption play-test:** the mod loads and the shard spawns. The shard is now deep red (Bloodstone model with a red tint, configurable look). The tint now covers every colour property, which also fixes glow colours staying unchanged.
- **Corruption plugin started (design phase 1):** new `Corruption` project with the corruption shard (drops from starred creatures and on first boss kills) and the Riftforge station. Shard drop rules are unit-tested; nothing has been tested in-game yet.
- Building with materials from nearby chests confirmed working in-game. Added `docs/multiplayer-test.md` for the two-player test.
- **Second dump analysed:** the biome fixes worked (913 items placed, 2 unplaced). Wisp fountains added to the producer rule. Reference data in `data/game-dump-1.0.20/` replaced with this corrected dump.
- **Third play-test:** the search box, the grouping button and crafting from nearby chests all work in-game. Category headers now line up with the recipe icons and use the recipe names' font size, and there's a new `HeaderFontSize` setting.
- **First in-game dump analysed** (Valheim 1.0.20). Biome detection works for the main materials. Fixed the gaps it showed: spawn entries that apply everywhere, boss phases, creature-only tool tiers (Bloodgold), eggs, wisps and trader quest keys. Details under data-driven biome detection. The dump's reference data is saved in `data/game-dump-1.0.20/`.
- **Second play-test:** the inventory and crafting screens work, categories collapse and expand, and `co_dump` writes its files. The search box didn't appear (see its known issues). Fixed the likely cause and stopped header labels from wrapping. Chest crafting and biome grouping haven't been tested yet.
- **First play-test (Valheim 1.0.20): the inventory and crafting screens were broken** (empty panels, placeholder text, no mouse cursor) and `co_dump` failed. Cause: the biome code used C# tuples, which need `System.ValueTuple.dll`, and Valheim doesn't ship it. The first recipe-list update crashed inside the game's own "open inventory" code. Fixed by removing the tuples. All features stay "Built, untested" until re-tested.
- The build now fails if the mod references an assembly Valheim doesn't ship (`System.ValueTuple`, `System.Memory`, `System.Buffers`).
- Mod code running inside game methods (recipe list, search bar, biome detection, chest counting and taking) now catches its own errors: it logs them once and falls back to vanilla behaviour instead of breaking the game's UI.

### 2026-10-15
- Added the Corruption mod design (`docs/corruption-design.md`) and its technical review (`docs/corruption-design-review.md`), and linked them from the planned Loot system and Combat mechanics entries. No mod code changed.

### 2026-09-29
- Biome grouping now works out material biomes from the game's own data (vegetation, spawns, locations and dungeon rooms, stations, crops, recipes), replacing the hand-written material list. Covers the Deep North and modded items. Added an Ocean group.
- `BiomeMaterialOverrides` is now only for corrections; it no longer extends a built-in list.
- Added `co_dump` (game data to CSV) and `co_biome` (explain an item's biome) console commands.
- Added `CraftingOverhaul.Tests` with unit tests for the biome rules.
- Added `docs/coop-networking.md` on co-op lag and desync. No mod code changed for this.

### 2026-09-28
- Added FEATURES.md to track features, status, config and patch targets.

### 2026-09-27: 0.1.0
- Turned the Jötunn mod stub into CraftingOverhaul (SDK-style project, JotunnLib 2.30.2, publicized game assemblies).
- Added the recipe search box, collapsible recipe categories (by item type or biome) and crafting/building from nearby chests. Compiled against current game reference assemblies; not yet tested in-game.
